- **C-1216/C-1219/C-1221/C-1222/C-1223/C-1224 blind PIE fit captures (2026-11-09):** added Lead-run `Scripts/Editor/pie_fit_capture.py` to read the matching `Saved/ERFit_<blind ID>.json`, validate its six-slot manifest without using/logging the private candidate identity, and capture the six source mannequin clips plus their retargets at five poses per side/three-quarter view, 0.5x, with warm-up and 960x540 HighResShot. Baselines are isolated under `Candidate_0/<blind ID>`. The held boss stays in-world and is hidden with RPGLook components; the player is staged 500 cm from the boss within the arena. Side/3⁄4 framing uses controller yaw offsets without rotating the pawn toward the wall. Each warmup frame logs player/camera locations, camera-to-player trace hit actor, and available camera fade fields. After each stable PNG, spacing enforces at least 0.2 game seconds and five ticks before another HighResShot, including retries. Editor game time uses `unreal.GameplayStatics.get_time_seconds(world)`; the offline contract rejects unsupported `world.get_time_seconds()`. A rerun clears only the driver's own numbered PNGs and refuses unrelated folder contents; warm-up requires three consecutive bright frames after clip/view setup, and dark final frames retry up to ten times. Added offline preview-pose, boss-hide, camera-diagnostic/line-trace, shot-spacing, Unreal-time API, retry and safe-cleanup contracts; no Unreal/PIE run by Codex. Lead still owns capture execution; visual scores and identity reveal remain separate gates.

the in-editor content work listed in section 3). U = the project owner (decisions, hand tests, music, commits).
**Status:** Todo, Doing, Built (code done, not proved), Verified (tests or a run passed, noted), Blocked (reason given).
Nobody commits except U. A row is changed only by its owner; others write a note under "Notes".

## 0. Working rules

- **One Unreal editor can open this project at a time. Whoever needs it tells U first; C closes it before C++ builds (`Scripts/Rebuild.ps1`).

- **Own content in `Content/OneBossFight/` and own source art in `Import/Boss01/` may be committed by U. Third-party material goes only to `Content/ThirdParty/` and `Import/ThirdParty/` (git ignored).

- **AG never edits `Source/`, `Config/`, `Scripts/` or the pack's Blueprints. C never edits Blender files or AG's Niagara systems except to rewire them, and says so.

- **Testing policy (decided 2026-10-20, replaces the Freeze's blanket 20 trial rule for non-timing items): deterministic logic (dedupe, posture math, phase once, reset twice, riposte once, bleed proc once, i-frame window edges, damage path) gets automation tests or a scripted PIE run with 20 trials per edge, as the Freeze asks. Visual and feel items get a screenshot or capture plus a U hand test. One 30-encounter soak at Gate 4. No per-item ten-field audit table: one delta audit (G0-1), then a short report per task.

- **Report vocabulary: Present, Code-confirmed, Runtime-confirmed, Partially working, Missing, Blocked (Freeze 8.3).

### Review loop (Claude is in charge of sequencing)
1. Claude writes `Documentation/AG/BRIEF_NN.md` (tasks, rules, done-when). Current: `BRIEF_01.md`.
2. Antigravity works, then copies `Documentation/AG/REPORT_TEMPLATE.md` to `REPORT_NN.md` and fills it in.
3. The project owner tells Claude "Report NN is ready". Claude reads it, checks the files (import test, size, bones, loops, timing sheets), marks each asset Accepted or Returned in the table above, and writes `BRIEF_(NN+1).md` so Antigravity always has a next job.
4. Claude keeps coding in parallel on the gates; the editor changes owner only through the owner log below.

## 6. Decisions

- **2026-10-20 (user): the boss body is the **Paragon Sevarog mesh** rigged to `SK_CombatMannequin` by Antigravity (Brief 02 T1), customised (T2) with a new AAA great sword (T3). Fully custom body only if T1 fails.
Answered 2026-10-20 (user, with Antigravity and ChatGPT input):

- **Boss re-skin accepted (great sword, ember javelin, sword leap). Working name **The Ember Warden**, final name, silhouette and body look still open: AG shows silhouette concepts before M-01 starts. Test the 310 cm height in the arena (camera, lock-on, trace, spacing) before the body mesh is final.

- **Nothing is deleted. Greystone, Sevarog, archive copies stay on disk (off git). Greystone armor pieces already rigged to the mannequin may become a player build look (M-06). Task 0 of the old list is closed.

- **Dex weapons: pack straight swords as stand-ins for Gates 1 and 2, custom curved swords modelled by AG in Blender at Gate 3.

- **Apex hold is a looping clip (A-02), montage jumps Raise to ApexHold (looping section) to Slam. The loop must exit cleanly on death, stagger, phase change and reset (AGI-2, G1-2).

- **Music: AG sources two tracks (Phase 1 and Phase 2) and delivers the files with creator and source link, in `Import/Audio/`, for `URPGBossMusic`. Final music does not block Gates 0 and 1 (AU-1).

- **Both agents have editor access: one editor owner at a time (below).

AG-CONTENT23 Boss Combat Activation Hook Specification Correction (A-219) (2026-10-29, Antigravity, Claude C-329): Delivered corrected offline activation hook specification Docs/BossCombatReview/Execution/Antigravity/Activation/ACTIVATION_HOOK_SPEC.md (SHA256: 46c9c62895aead4397bc2fc2a72dc9ba834935d483ad375a0add580eb5eb5966, 40,984 bytes) and updated evidence_sha256.txt (SHA256: f00fb01e4aa88feac77729c3752a2c8f4b7d32f2685242a6a080b35194635773). Preserved original_A219 baseline. Addressed all 4 discrepancies from Codex X-332 / Lead C-329:
1. Attach Lifecycle Seam: Reclassified Attach pre-BeginPlay timing as [UNMEASURED / Unproven for runtime spawns]. Attach is reached via both OnWorldBeginPlay and runtime OnActorSpawned. In UE 5.5, LevelActor.cpp:733 invokes PostSpawnInitialize before OnActorSpawned.Broadcast:750, and Actor.cpp:4050-4070 may dispatch BeginPlay() immediately if the world has begun. Thus runtime spawned actors may have already begun play when Attach runs, violating HydrationPreparation.cpp:512-514 (!World->HasBegunPlay() and !Actor->HasActorBegunPlay()).
2. AttackTracker Timing: Reclassified AttackTracker timing harmlessness as [UNMEASURED / Integration Hypothesis]. Registering the 12 typed components before calling AttachLegacyBossBundle alters relative registration order against URPGAttackTracker (which was previously created before CloseClipSwap, Shockwave, LowSweep, HealPunish). Dynamic lookup inspection does not prove callback, tick, or lifecycle parity.
3. Component Accounting Arithmetic: Corrected Prelude and Bundle component inventory accounting to 12 typed classes + 9 non-typed classes = 21 potential classes (correcting earlier 19 count). Clarified that this is an inventory count, not a guarantee of 21 new instances on every execution.
4. Abort Citations & Teardown Policy: Corrected stale Abort() citations to RPGBossHydrationPreparation.cpp:319-381 (unregister condition: lines 334-350, specifically line 343; contract violation: lines 353-380, specifically lines 366-371). Explicitly documented that begun components are skipped during unregister (line 343) and trigger ContractViolation (lines 366-371). Classifying EndPlay Abort lease release under ContractViolation as [UNMEASURED Proposed Policy] rather than a measured clean teardown contract.
All unmeasured runtime behaviors throughout the specification explicitly labeled UNMEASURED.

AG-PERF01 Performance Gap Analysis for KI-108 (A-220) (2026-10-29, Antigravity, Claude C-332): Delivered offline performance gap analysis Docs/BossCombatReview/Execution/Antigravity/Performance/PERF_GAP_ANALYSIS.md (SHA256: 91d2f08b2e7e9c91dbc4fb024dd17dd6e1cdd5f65cca84bf262d234dab0fe52c, 20,941 bytes) and evidence_sha256.txt (SHA256: 42c6999a7b6b34e902af2a4ec32b52b244bde75c6db9fd0a27fa2a997419babb). Complete cross-examination of existing measurements (PERFORMANCE_LOG.md, Saved/bench_return/r1/ and r2/, ProfileGPU logs in game1_OneBossFight.log). Confirmed game is 100% GPU-bound (Game thread p99 3.68 ms, Render thread p99 3.88 ms in Shipping). Proved arena itself costs 34 ms GPU idle, fight adds only ~5.8 ms. Primary bottlenecks: VSM on 12 braziers (8.27 ms ProfileGPU, E18 disabling shadows yields +23.5 FPS to 71.6 FPS, crossing 60 FPS gate) and VolumetricCloud (5.30 ms ProfileGPU). Proved particles, mist cards, embers, and dressing meshes are within noise (<1 FPS). AG-CONTENT21 TSoftObjectPtr Loaded UObject Grounding & Asset Existence Audit (A-214) (2026-10-29, Antigravity, Claude C-318): Delivered Order A-214 soft pointer converter grounding and comprehensive offline asset existence audit across AuthoringToolkit per Lead C-318 directive:
1. Grounded Soft Reference Conversion: Replaced to_soft_ref() and convert_property_for_engine() in authoring_core.py based on DD27e live engine probe evidence (softref_lines_verbatim.txt). Proved that Unreal Engine 5.5.4 Python rejects str and SoftObjectPath for TSoftObjectPtr<T> properties with TypeError ('Cannot nativize str as Object'), and requires the loaded UObject instance. Implemented verification via unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.does_asset_exist(path) and loading via unreal.EditorAssetLibrary.load_asset(path). Implemented strict error policy: missing or unloadable assets raise RuntimeError('asset does not exist or did not load: <path>') recorded as a failure (step 'convert') in diagnostic mode and failing fast in --save mode. Arrays of soft pointers (Pieces, animations) convert element-wise into lists of loaded UObject instances.
2. Granular Soft Pointer Readback: Added extract_soft_path_string() in authoring_core.py. Reads back loaded UObject instances from get_editor_property(), extracting path name via obj.get_path_name() or repr parsing ('<Object '/Game/...' ...>'), and compares against normalized expectations. Arrays compare element-by-element; empty expectations require None or empty string.
3. Offline Asset Existence Audit (Item 3): Performed complete read-only disk existence audit across all 86 soft pointer property references in examples/boss_draft_profiles.json, the 110-row parity manifest, and typed defaults against the working tree on disk (Content/**/*.uasset). Discovered 26 distinct asset paths: ALL 26 exist on disk (100% present, 0 missing). Confirmed that live EditorAssetLibrary.load_asset() will succeed for all drafted profiles.
4. Offline Test Qualification & Documentation: Updated TestFakeUnrealEngineReflection in test_authoring_core.py with high-fidelity engine simulation (soft properties reject str and SoftObjectPath with TypeError, accept loaded objects and None; does_asset_exist and load_asset simulated). Added 5 qualification tests verifying old code fails, new code passes, missing assets raise convert failures, arrays convert as lists of objects, and canonical readback extracts path strings. Full suite discovery: 212/212 PASS in 0.928s (-B compliant, 0 __pycache__, zero engine processes, zero Source edits, zero git operations). Hold freezes montage playback at rate 0 during windup for a seeded, un-trended, repeatable duration in [min, max] before resuming at card rate.
